================================================================================
HALO 3 BLENDER MATERIAL & SHADER COLLECTIONS REFERENCE GUIDE
================================================================================
Generated for: Halo 3 Modding (H3EK / Halo Asset Blender Development Toolset)
Shader Collections File: C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\H3EK\tags\levels\shader_collections.txt

HOW TO NAME MATERIALS IN BLENDER FOR HALO 3:
1. Format: [prefix] [shader_name]
   Example: For multi level Chill, prefix is 'chill' in shader_collections.txt.
   Shader tag: blue_panel_light.shader
   -> In Blender Material Name: 'chill blue_panel_light'

2. Default Prefix (*):
   If no prefix is provided, Halo tool looks in 'levels\shared\shaders\'.

3. Special Materials & Symbols:
   +sky      : Ceiling / Sky dome geometry (renders the scenario skybox)
   !portal   : Cluster portal geometry (occlusion / vis cluster boundary)
   %[name]   : Two-sided surface / weather mesh
   [name]'   : Water collision / surface tag

4. Box Canvas Setup:
   Floor     : mpshare mp_sand   (or boxcanvas sal_floor_tile / chill chill_ground_floor)
   Ceiling   : +sky
   Walls     : mpshare mp_sand   (or boxcanvas chill_wall01 / river riverworld_metal)
